B63D  11 00 B6      LD DE,DATA_B600
B640  06 00         LD B,00H

B645  20 5A         JR NZ,LB6A1
B647  11 00 B6      LD DE,DATA_B600

B67F  11 00 01      LD DE,0100H
B682  19            ADD HL,DE
B683  22 03 B6      LD (DATA_B603),HL
B686  11 00 B6      LD DE,DATA_B600
B689  CD 39 44      CALL $WRITE             ; write a record to an open file
B68C  28 D3         JR Z,LB661

B69A  36 20         LD (HL),20H
B69C  ED B0         LDIR
B69E  C3 2D 40      JP $JP2DOS              ; return to TRSDOS READY
